2a4(rgyud): cad kyi sems dang /spyod pa'i rjes su 'jug pa'i chos bstan pa sna tshogs mnyam pa nyid snyan cing rgya che la zab pa dang /spobs pa dang /cho 'phrul dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing du mar de bzhin gshegs

2b1(rgyud): dag la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag brgyad bcu rtsa bzhi dang /nyan thos chen po rab tu mang po thams cad kyang dgra bcom pa/zag pa zad pa/srid par kun du sbyor 
2b2(rgyud): ba chad pa/yang dag pa'i shes pas sems shin du rnam par grol ba/rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs dang /cho 'phrul bsam gyis mi khyab pas rnam par 'phrul ba'i mthu chen po thob pa/lta ba chags pa med pa/thams

2b4(rgyud): ka bi na dang /tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor dang /tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po dang /tshe dang ldan pas skul byed dang /tshe dang ldan pa dga' byed dang /tshe dang ldanapa ga ya 'od srung dang /tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung 
2b5(rgyud): chen po dang /tshe dang ldanapa lteng rgyas 'od srung dang /de dag la sogs pa nyan thos chen po rab tu mang po dang /lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po la sogs pa gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu grangs med dpag tu 
2b6(rgyud): med gzhal du med pa dag dang /mi 'dzed kyi bdag po tshangs pa dang /lha'i bu tshangs pa bzang po la sogs pa tshangs ris kyi lha'i bu rnams dang /lha'i bu rab 'thab bral g.yog 'thab bral gyi lha'i ris kyi 
2b7(rgyud): lha'i bu dang bcas pa dang /yongs su dga' ldan dang /'phrul dga' dang /gzhan 'phrul dbang byed dang /lha'i dbang po brgya byin lha'i bu thams cad kyi 'khor can dang /lha ma yin gyi dbang po thag bzangs 
2b8(rgyud): ris dang /stobs can dang rab sim dang /sgrag can dang rnam par snang byed dang /de la sogs pa lha ma yin gyi dbang po tshad med/grangs med dpag tu med pa rnams dang /klu'i rgyal po rgya mtsho dang /ma dros pa

4a1(rgyud): @#//sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang ni ldan/bdud dang phas kyis rgol ba dang /dran pa'i tshogs can gyi tshogs thams cad ni bcom/brjod pa'i sgra dang tshigs su bcad pa ni 'phags

4a6(rgyud): phreng ba dang ldan pa'i snam rten dang bcas pa/rin po che du ma'i pad ma'i snying po la chags pa'i rin po che ke ke ru dang /ke ke ru chen po dang /an da rnyil dang /mthon ka chen po dang /dun las du ma'i 'od zer 'bar bas 
4a7(rgyud): snang bar gyur bas kun tu mdzes pa/rin po che dang /rdo rje thur mas brgyan pa'i gdugs yu bas bstod pa bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag du mas grib mar bsgribs shing bskyabs pa/dpag bsam gyi shing du mas mdzes

5a1(rgyud): @#//gnas dang /yi dgas kyi skye gnas dang /gshin rje'i 'jig rten rnams kyi ming yang mi drag par gyur to//'jig rten gyi bar gang dag mun pa mun nag dang /mun pa mun nag chen por 'dug pa de dag thams 
5a2(rgyud): cad kyang gsal bar snang bar gyur to//der sems can gang dag skyes pa de dag thams cad dang /sems can gzhan 'dir skyes pa rnams kyang gcig gis gcig shes so//de nas sems can de dag thams cad kyi 
5a3(rgyud): ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i chos kyi dga' ba thob ste/bde ba mchog gis tshim par gyur nas kun tu bltas pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mthong ste/de dag gis gser 
5a4(rgyud): gyi mchod sdong ltar dpal gyis 'bar zhing rang gi gzi brjid kyis mngon par 'phags pa de mthong nas de nyid la skyabs su dong ngo //skyabs su dong ma thag du de dag thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil

5b1(rgyud): kyis rje bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes smras nas/rang rang gi gnas su dong ste/lag na mtshon cha sna tshogs can/gos sna tshogs bgos pa/cha byed dang rgyan sna tshogs can/mche ba mi bzad par 'jigs su rung ba bzhin 
5b2(rgyud): mi sdug par bsgyur ba/mig dang bzhin dang /skra kun du 'bar zhing rab tu 'bar ba dag skad cig de nyid la gcig tu 'dus nas bdud sdig can ga la ba der dong ste phyin nas bdud sdig can la 'di skad ces smras so//
5b3(rgyud): bzhengs bzhengs thog mar bzhud la ji tsam du dge sbyong gau ta ma nyan thos dang /byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun dang bcas pa/de ci nas kyang phyis bdag cag gi yul du gnod pa cung zad kyang mi bskyed pa dang /bdag

5b7(rgyud): bu dang /rdo rje rtse gcig pa dang /mnga' bo che dang /mdud thud dang /rtse gsum pa dang /ral gri dang 'khor lo dang /gzhu dang mnga' la sogs pa'i mtshon cha lag na thogs shing bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste skad

6a1(rgyud): @#//snang ngo//bdud kyi sgra de dag thams cad kyang sangs rgyas kyi sgra dang /chos kyi sgra dang dge 'dun gyi sgra dang /yang dag par 'gro ba'i sgra rnams su grag go/bdud sdig can ni zhags pa lnga'i bcings bas

6b1(rgyud): gang gis 'dzud pa dang/dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su 'gro bar byed pa yang ston cig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/des ngan 'gro log par ltung bar 'gro ba'i phyir ro//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang/gsang ba ba'i 
6b2(rgyud): bdag po lag na rdo rjes/bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' mnyan nas rin po che'i pad ma'i rdo rje'i stan las langs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas/bus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga rin po che'i pad ma'i snying po 
6b3(rgyud): la btsugs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/bcom ldan 'das la tshul dang 'dra ba'i tshigs su gcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstong do//sems can kun gyi nyon mongs 
6b4(rgyud): pa//gzhig pa'i slad du 'dren pa byung //khyod ni bdud rnams kun las rgyal//'jig rten rtsa lag gcig bu khyod//thar pa'i grong khyer 'jug pa'i lam//ye shes ma lus khyod gcig bus//yang dag 
6b5(rgyud): brnyas nas thugs rdza yis//brlan pa'i thugs kyis bstan pa mdzad//khyod sngon sbyin dang tshul khrims dang //bzod dang brtson 'grus bsam gtan dang //shes rab la sogs yon tan gyis//bdag 
6b6(rgyud): mdzad slad kyis sems can btsud//khyod kyis chos rnams skye med par//bshad cing de bzhin rnam phye yang //'bras bur dogs pa ma mchis pa//kye ma mgon po khyod kyis bshad//'khor lo yan lag 
6b7(rgyud): bcu gnyis pa//khyod nyid mkhyen nas rab tu bskor//mu stegs can dang bdud btul te//chos kyi snang ba gsal bar mdzad//nyon mongs brag chen rnam kun tu//ye shes rdo rjes rab bcom nas//sus 
6b8(rgyud): kyang thob par ma gyur pa'i//byang chub bla na med pa brnyes//gang gi mtshan yang rdzogs pa yi//byang chub sa bon nyid 'gyur pa//khyod ni 'jig rten mchog gcig bu//dam pa rab sbyin bdag po lags//
7a1(rgyud): @#//khyod ni mgon po khyod ni pha//khyod skyabs khyod ni dpung gnyen lags//thugs rje chen po ye shes nyid//mya ngan 'das gnas gcig pu khyod//khyod nyid gshegs nas sems can rnams//sems 
7a2(rgyud): can dmyal las rnam par grol//rang gi sku yang yongs btang nas//khyod kyis srog chags rnams kyang bsrungs//lus can kun gyis ma thob pa'i//ye shes khyod kyis brnyes gyur nas//grol te 'jig 
7a3(rgyud): rten rnams kyang 'grol//kye ma khyod ni rtsa lag bzang //mgon po khyod kyi 'od zer gyi//dra bas 'jig rten khams rnams khyab//mya ngan 'das las lhag pa bde//lus can kun gyis thob par mdzad//khyod 
7a4(rgyud): kyi 'od kyi gzis bcom bas//gza' rnams kun dang dgung zla dang //gdugs rnams dang ni zhugs rnams dang //nor bu lha mer ma gyur to//de nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rjes chu la dang 'dra ba'i
